# Missing inputs

What the library should hold but does not yet, why, where to get it and what to do next. Generated from the `gaps` field
of each card in catalog/technologies/ and from register entries without a card. Agents: call `list_gaps`.

905 missing inputs on 286 cards; 0 register technologies have no card yet.

| not_in_sources_read | 660 |
| general_knowledge_only | 215 |
| source_unreachable | 15 |
| paid_source | 12 |
| status_unchecked | 3 |

| [Allam-Fetvedt cycle (oxy-combustion supercritical CO2 power)](technologies-CCS.md#ccs-allam-cycle) | whether any utility-scale Allam-Fetvedt cycle plant is still planned (status) | NET Power's March 2026 update describes its first commercial project as gas turbines with post-combustion capture; it does not say when or whether an Allam-cycle plant follows. RESOLVED NEGATIVE 2026-10-28: no utility-scale Allam-cycle plant is under construction or firmly planned. NET Power is running demonstration-plant testing campaigns through 2026 (FY2025 10-K, Mar 2024), and in January 2026 withdrew the interconnection request for its proposed Midcontinent clean power hub (Energy Intelligence); the MIT CCS project database lists the 295 MW scale-up as cancelled/inactive.
